**Q: How does Decorator improve the efficiency or structure of an application?**
- [ ] By completely rewriting the code automatically.
- [x] By providing optimized patterns and reducing redundancy compared to basic approaches.
- [ ] By removing all errors from the console.
- [ ] By converting all code to binary.

*Answer/Solution:* While Decorator is powerful, misusing it can lead to memory leaks or unexpected behavior.

### Python Programming - Hard
**Q: At a low-level architectural scale, how does GIL resolve complex system bottlenecks?**
- [x] By leveraging advanced algorithms, concurrent processing, or deep architectural abstractions to maximize performance.
- [ ] By simply restarting the server.
- [ ] By adding more RAM to the instance.
- [ ] By alerting the user.
*Answer/Solution:* GIL requires a deep understanding of computer science principles to implement and debug correctly.

**Q: In a highly distributed, high-throughput environment, what guarantees does GIL provide?**
- [ ] No guarantees.
- [ ] It guarantees absolute zero latency.
- [x] It provides strict consistency, fault tolerance, or specialized optimizations critical for scaling.
- [ ] It replaces the need for a database entirely.
*Answer/Solution:* Mastery of GIL is essential for building resilient, enterprise-grade distributed systems.

**Q: How does Caching improve the efficiency or structure of an application?**

**Q: At a low-level architectural scale, how does CAP Theorem resolve complex system bottlenecks?**
- [x] By leveraging advanced algorithms, concurrent processing, or deep architectural abstractions to maximize performance.
- [ ] By simply restarting the server.
- [ ] By adding more RAM to the instance.
- [ ] By alerting the user.
*Answer/Solution:* CAP Theorem requires a deep understanding of computer science principles to implement and debug correctly.

**Q: In a highly distributed, high-throughput environment, what guarantees does CAP Theorem provide?**
- [ ] No guarantees.
- [ ] It guarantees absolute zero latency.
- [x] It provides strict consistency, fault tolerance, or specialized optimizations critical for scaling.
- [ ] It replaces the need for a database entirely.
*Answer/Solution:* Mastery of CAP Theorem is essential for building resilient, enterprise-grade distributed systems.

**Q: How does IaC improve the efficiency or structure of an application?**
- [ ] By completely rewriting the code automatically.
- [x] By providing optimized patterns and reducing redundancy compared to basic approaches.
- [ ] By removing all errors from the console.
- [ ] By converting all code to binary.
*Answer/Solution:* IaC is an intermediate technique designed to streamline and optimize processes.

**Q: What is a common pitfall to avoid when implementing IaC?**
- [x] Using it too frequently without understanding its underlying performance costs or lifecycle impacts.
- [ ] Not paying for its premium license.
- [ ] Typing it in lowercase.
- [ ] Using it on a Tuesday.
*Answer/Solution:* While IaC is powerful, misusing it can lead to memory leaks or unexpected behavior.

**Q: How does React Native improve the efficiency or structure of an application?**
- [ ] By completely rewriting the code automatically.

*Answer/Solution:* While React Native is powerful, misusing it can lead to memory leaks or unexpected behavior.
